# The golden trees, 2026-11-22

The per-module tables of every golden tree of issue [#23], as
`scripts/python/refusal_report.py` printed them from the runs that
[`../performance.md`][performance] summarizes: the four `Init` fragments
and the nine corpus entries whose closures the checker gets through, each
translated by a copy of the translator at `5cfd6f5`, run through the
refusal loop with `agda_check.py --refusals --messages -j 4` and checked
fresh in its final state with `agda_check.py --fresh -j 4 --json`, under
Lean v4.33.1 and Agda 2.8.0, on a 20-thread Intel i9-12900H with 62 GB of
memory that other sessions shared.  [`../performance.md`][performance]
defines the columns and the classes; the three largest closures, which
the loop does not get through, are there too, under issue [#94].

## When each tree ran

Times are wall seconds; the load averages are the one, five and fifteen
minute averages of `/proc/loadavg` when each step began and ended.

## The three largest closures, as far as the loop got

The same tables for `finset-card-image`, `subgroup-card-dvd` and
`real-sqrt-nonneg`, whose loops stopped before they ended
([`../performance.md`][performance], "The three largest closures", and
[#94]).  A module the loop never reached has no listing, and its values
count as unchecked; a final check exists for none of them, so the `Agda s`
and `Agda MiB` columns are those of a check of the loop's last text,
which is not a final state.  The loops ran at load averages of 2 to 5,
and `real-sqrt-nonneg`'s alongside the other two.

### Summary

| Tree | Constants | Modules | MB | Translate s | Translator refused | Values | Checked | Refused | Behind a refused type | Types refused | Loop Agda s | Loop MiB | Final wall s | Final MiB | Result |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `finset-card-image` | 3,367 | 125 | 37.28 | 7.4 | 0 | 3,849 | 3,656 | 116 (#26 1, #27 5, #31 5, #89 6, unclassified 99) | 0 | 0 | 3,176 | 8,631 | 0.0 | 0 | modules the loop did not reach |
| `subgroup-card-dvd` | 6,918 | 322 | 56.88 | 6.8 | 0 | 7,139 | 6,407 | 274 (#26 1, #27 11, #31 5, #89 48, unclassified 209) | 6 (unclassified 6) | 2 | 3,731 | 8,578 | 0.0 | 0 | modules the loop did not reach |
| `real-sqrt-nonneg` | 11,036 | 511 | 125.87 | 23.1 | 0 | 12,725 | 10,807 | 711 (#26 1, #27 10, #31 5, #89 75, unclassified 620) | 285 (unclassified 285) | 8 | 20,016 | 8,559 | 0.0 | 0 | modules the loop did not reach |
